NAVAJO COUNTY BOARD OF SUPERVISORS’ MINUTES 
TUESDAY, May 8, 2012  
 
PRESENT: David Tenney, Supervisor; Jesse Thompson, Supervisor; Jonathan M. Nez, Supervisor; Jerry 
Brownlow, Vice-Chairman; and J.R. DeSpain, Chairman were present.  
9:08 a.m.  Call To Order: Pledge of Allegiance and Invocation was led by Supervisor Nez.  
CALL TO THE PUBLIC: There were no comments from the public.   
CONSENT AGENDA:  a) Constable Reports: Winslow Precinct #2, March 2012  b) Justice Court Reports: 
Winslow Precinct #2, April; Snowflake Taylor Precinct #3, April; Kayenta Precinct #4, April; and Show Low 
Precinct #5, April 2012 c) Warrant & Voucher Report over $1000 April, 2012  d) Intergovernmental Agreement 
for law enforcement services between Coconino County and Navajo County  e) Agreement Number 120067DC 
between Arizona Department of Corrections Training Academy and Sheriff’s Office for correctional officer 
certification training f) Authorization to re-issue check g) Conduct precinct-based election for Joseph City Fire 
District on November 6, 2012 to elect 2 positions with a 4 year term beginning December 1, 2012 and ending 
November 30, 2016 h) Conduct precinct-based election for Linden Fire District on November 6, 2012 to el ect 2 
positions with a 4 year term beginning January 1, 2013 and ending December 31, 2016  i) Property transfer of 
three file cabinets from the Public Fiduciary to Woodruff Water and Fire District j) Assessment and Tax Roll 
Correction: Resolution #9376 & #9377 k) Traffic control changes along White Antelope Road (South) from the 
intersection of Concho Highway and White Antelope Road (South) to North of Lawson Lane and approve the 
Traffic Analysis l) Traffic control changes at the intersection of Oak Tree Drive and Indian Trail and to approve 
the Stop Sign Analysis  m) Sheriff Office 3 year computer lease contract with Zions First Nation al Bank 
(National Bank) for 53 Dell and 8 HP Computers with accessories  n) Sympathy letter to San Juan County 
Commission, County Administrator Rick Bailey & Family: Supervisor Brownlow made a motion to authorize 
the Chairman to sign the items in the consent agenda as presented; motion seconded by Supervisor Thompson. 
Supervisor Nez discussed item n expressing his sympathies to Mr . Bailey for the loss of his wife. Vote 
unanimous approving the motion.  
BOARD OF SUPERVISORS:  
a. RSC Rental Corporation  and Navajo County Recognizing Excellence : Melissa Buckley  and Debra 
Chapman recognized Jayla Johnstun for her exceptional help in the mai lroom. Jayla is an employee of 
the Assessor’s office.  
b. Personnel Service Awards : David Coolidge  recognized the following employees for their years of 
service to Navajo County: Deputy David Hummer,5 years; Sgt. Jack Arend, 5 years; Mariclare Hannah, 
5 years; Terry Kent Brewer , 5 years; Michael Sand oval, 5 years; Donna Hurstrom, 5 years; Christine 
Frayer, 5 years; Bonnie Erickson, 10 years; Geneva Durkee 15 years.  
HUMAN RESOURCES: Consideration and possible approval of Personnel Actions: David Coolidge reviewed 
the 11 item personnel action report. Supervisor Tenney made a motion to authorize the Chairman to sign the 
personnel action report as presented;  motion seconded by Supervisor Brownlow . Supervisor Thompson and 
Chairman DeSpain thanked David for the job he is doing. Vote unanimous approving the motion.  
NAVAJO COUNTY PUBLIC HEALTH SERVICES DISTRICT Board of Directors Session : Supervisor 
Thompson made a motion to enter into the Navajo County Public Health Services District Board of Directors

<!-- page 2 -->
Board of Supervisor Minutes 05-08-2012 
Page 2 of 4 
 
Session; motio n seconded by Supervisor Nez. Vote unanimous approving the motion. Consideration and 
possible approval of Contract Number ADHS12 -021336, Population Health Policy Initiative : Dr. Kartchn er 
discussed the item and a nswered questions of the Board. Supervisor Thompson made a motion  to approve 
Contract Number ADHS 12 -021336; motion seconded by Supervisor Brownlow. Vote unanimous approving 
the motion. Supervisor Tenney made a motion to return to regular session; motion seconded by Supervisor 
Thompson. Vote unanimous approving the motion.  
COUNTY ATTORNEY: Consideration and possible approval to authorize the Navajo County Attorney’s 
Office to retain a Media Consultant to assist in managing media on a criminal case : Brad Carlyon discussed the 
item. He indicated that Navajo County has received numerous hate emails , phone calls and voice mail 
messages. Mr. Carlyon requested that the Board approve the contract with David Leibowitz  in the amount of 
$10,000.00. Supervisor Tenney made a motion to approve a contract with Dav id Leibowitz in the amount of 
$10,000.00 to retain a Media Consultant to assist in managing media on a criminal case; motion seconded by 
Supervisor Thompson. Vote unanimous approving the motion.  
EMERGENCY MANAGEMENT: Emergency Management Update on Wildfir e Preparedness: Dan Hinz and 
Mary Springer give a brief presentation on Emergency preparedness. Alison Antoni and Kirk Webb  gave a 
presentation on White Mountain Fire and Life Safety (WMFLS). Ms. Antoni also gave a presentation on Ready 
Set Go help aid for fire departments and surrounding agencies in educating residence living in WUI how better 
to prepare, protect, and survive in the event of another Wild L and Fire. Kirk Webb, Fire Marshall gave some 
additional information. Mr. Jayne acknowledged Mary Sprin ger, Dan Hinz and Catrina Roe and thanked M s. 
Antoni and Mr. Webb for their presentation.  
FINANCE: Public Hearing: Long Term Obligation Presentation : James Menlove  discussed the item. Mr. 
Menlove discussed remodeling of the Navajo County Jail and analysis  of a proposal to incur long term 
indebtedness. Mark Reader  with Stone & Youngberg is present along with Sheriff Clark, Commander Garcia 
and Commander Powell. No comments have been made either for or against this item sent to the C lerk of the 
Board. Mr. Menlove spoke about the b enefits of the detention facility expansion project and the jail space . He 
discussed the medical, kitchen and laundry room facilities. Mr. Menlove showed a site plan of the expansion 
project and the jail facility layout. Commander Ga rcia spoke of the security features of the site plan and 
remaining ADA compliant. Mr. Jayne made comments regarding the current situation. Chairman DeSpain  
asked about the fencing around the area. Commander Garcia spoke of the confinement areas. Discussion  took 
place regarding utilities, including more efficient equipment. Supervisor Thompson indicated that his concerns 
have been explained. Sheriff Clark thanked the team for their hard work. Mr. Menlove introduced Mr. Mark 
Reader who spoke about the financi al aspects of the expansion. Mr. Menlove spoke of some of the stipulations 
to the financing including that the County does not have to have a reserve fund with the new funding. He 
indicated that the County will finance less and pay less interest. The interest rate is a little over 4%. The balance 
is right around 4.7 million of a 6.6 million dollar bond in 2008. Discussion is held. Mr. Menlove continued with 
the presentation. Mr. Reader addressed the B oard regarding the financing of the loan.  He discussed t he bid by 
Wells Fargo and older bonds. Mr. Reader answered questions of the Board. Chairman DeSpain opened the 
public hearing, there being no comments, closed the public hearing. Supervisor Brownlow reminded the public 
that if they want to comment to please send any comments to the Clerk of the Board. Supervisor Brownlow 
thanked Sheriff Clark for helping the Board with the debt service. Supervisor Thompson  asked that this 
information be shared with our communities.

<!-- page 3 -->
Board of Supervisor Minutes 05-08-2012 
Page 3 of 4 
 
PUBLIC WORKS:  
a. Consideration and possibl e approval of Task Agreement Number 3 to Memorandum of Agreement 
(AGN00090001) between Bureau of Indian Affairs, Navajo Region and Navajo County for the purchase 
of equipment parts and supplies for an amount not to exceed $38,000.00 from District I Special  Road 
Funds: Jeanine Carruthers discussed the item. Supervisor Nez made comments about the item. 
Supervisor Nez made a motion  to approve Task Agreement Number 3 to Memorandum of Agreement 
(AGN00090001) between Bureau of Indian Affairs, Navajo Region and Na vajo County for the purchase 
of equipment parts and supplies for an amount not to exceed $38,000.00 from District I Special Road 
Funds; motion seconded by Supervisor Thompson. Vote unanimous approving the motion.  
b. Consideration and possible award of Contra ct #B12-03-001 Hot Asphaltic Concrete Pavement Overlay 
to Grey Mountain Construction, LLC and Surface Contracting, Inc. : Bill Bess discussed the item. 
Supervisor Tenney made a motion to award Contract #B12-03-001 Hot Asphaltic Concrete Pavement 
Overlay to Grey Mountain Construction, LLC and Surface Contracting, Inc orporated; motion seconded 
by Supervisor Brownlow. Vote unanimous approving the motion.  
c. Consideration and possible approval to cancel award of Contract #B12 -01-007 Supplying and 
Delivering Liquid Asphalt Products to Ergon Asphalt & Emulsions, Inc. : Bill Bess discussed the item. 
Supervisor Brownlow made a motion  to cancel the award of  Contract #B12 -01-007 Supplying and 
Delivering Liquid Asphalt Products to Ergon Asphalt & Emulsions, Inc .; motion se conded by 
Supervisor Tenney . Vote unanimous approving the motion.  Supervisor Tenney  and Jimmy Jayne 
thanked Deputy County Attorney Alisa Schiebler for her partnership on this project.  
d. Consideration and possible approval of Task Agreement Number 4 to Memor andum of Agreement 
(AGN00090001) between Bureau of Indian Affairs, Navajo Region, Ft. Defiance Agency and Navajo 
County for an amount not to exceed $7,000 in District II Special Project Road Funds, $25,000 Secure 
Rural Schools Funds and $25,000 Navajo Hopi Partitioned Lands Funds (subject to grant approval) to be 
used for the purchase and delivery o f material to BIA Public Roads : Janine Carruthers discussed the 
item. Supervisor Thompson indicated that the item should be for the Navajo Hopi Indian Relocation 
Commission Funds. Supervisor Thompson made a motion  to approve Task Agreement Number 4 to 
the Memorandum of Agreement between Bureau of Indian Affairs, Navajo Region, Ft. Defiance Agency 
and Navajo County for an amount not to exceed $7,000 in District II Special Project Road Funds, 
$25,000 Secure Rural Schools Funds and $25,000 Navajo Hopi Indian Relocation Commission  to be 
used for the purchase and delivery of material to BIA Public Roads; motion seconded by  Supervisor 
Nez. Vote unanimous approving the motion.   
BOARD OF SUPERVISORS ACTING AS BOARD OF EQUALIZATION : Supervisor Tenney made a 
motion to enter into the Board of Equalization ; motion seconded by Supervisor Nez. Vote unanimous 
approving the motion.  
a. Swearing in of Assessor, Appraisers and Petition ers: Mr. Thomas Stack and Ms. Cammy Darris were 
sworn in.  
b. Discussion and possible action regarding Petition for Review of Taxpayer Notice of Claim APN 210-68-
098, 309-90-002, 309-90-004, 309-90-007, 309-90-010, 309-90-012, 309-90-017, 309-90-019, 309-90-
024, 309-90-025, 309-90-026, 309-90-027, 309-90-032, 309-90-033, 309-90-037, 309-90-038, 309-90-
040, 309-90-043, 309-90-044, 309-90-045, 309-90-047, 309-90-048, 309-90-049, 309-90-050, 309-90-
052, 309-90-054, 309-90-055, 309-90-057, 309-90-066, 309-90-067, 309-90-072, 309-90-074, 309-90-
075, 309-90-077, 309-90-081, 309-90-083, 309-90-088, 309-90-090, 309-90-091, 309-90-092, and 309-
90-095: Cammy Darris discussed the item. Mrs. Darris indicated that Snow Creek is a subdivision that is 
a completed unit ; unit t wo is not completed. M rs. Darris discussed Unit One  and indicated that the 
Notice of Claim can only be filed on factual verifiable error. Mrs. Darris indicated that there is no error.

<!-- page 4 -->
Board of Supervisor Minutes 05-08-2012 
Page 4 of 4 
 
Mr. Stack indicated that it does fall under the error correction statute . Discussion is held. Mrs. Darris 
and Mr. Stack answered questions of the Board. Supervisor Tenney  indicated that based on the 
information presented by the County A ssessor and the petition, he made a motion to deny the petition 
as to Unit One; motion secon ded by Supervisor Brownlow . Vote unanimous approving the motion. 
Discussion was held on Unit Two. The issue raised is that the Assessor’s Office used Unit One values to 
assess Unit Two. Discussion was held on Unit Two.  
Supervisor Nez left at 11: 50 a.m.  
Discussion continued regarding Unit Two. Mrs. Darris and Mr. Stack answered questions of the Board. 
Supervisor T enney made a motion  to deny petitioners claim in Unit T wo; motion seconded by 
Supervisor Thompson. Vote unanimous approving the motion. Supervisor Tenney made a motion to 
return to regular session; motion seconded by Supervisor Thompson. Vote unanimous approving the 
motion.   
COUNTY BUSINESS UPDATE: Report from Board members, County Attorney and County Manager : 
Supervisor Tenney reported on the le gislative session. Supervisor Thompson reported on several meetings 
regarding local res ources. Supervisor Brownlow  reported on the Scott Ranch Road project and the W hite 
Mountain Sheriff’s Posse ride scheduled for tomorrow. Chairman DeSpain thanked everyone for their get well 
wishes after his surgery.  
ADJOURN: At 12:20  p.m. Supervisor Thompson  made a motion to adjourn; motion seconded by  
Supervisor Brownlow. Vote unanimous approving the motion.  
 
 
APPROVED: 
 
__________________________________ 
J.R. DeSpain, Chairman 
 
ATTEST:  
 
DATE: July 24, 2012 
 
Melissa W. Buckley - Clerk of the Board
